Provider-specific fields needed for tool-call continuation are\n     5\tpreserved by the vendor integrations; nothing here can change team permissions.\n     6\t\"\"\"\n     7\t\n     8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     9\t\n    10\timport os\n    11\tfrom typing import Any\n    12\t\n    13\tfrom langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel\n    14\t\n    15\tfrom .models import ModelProfile, UserConfig\n    16\t\n    17\t\n    18\tclass ModelConfigError(RuntimeError):\n    19\t    pass\n    20\t\n    21\t\n    22\t#: protocols whose models do not advertise an `xhigh` reasoning level\n    23\tNO_XHIGH_PROTOCOLS = {\"deepseek\"}\n    24\t\n    25\t\n    26\tdef normalize_effort(profile: ModelProfile) -&gt; tuple[dict[str, Any], str | None]:\n    27\t    \"\"\"Map a requested reasoning effort onto what this provider can accept.\n    28\t\n    29\t    Rule (user decision 2026-09-12): when a model does not support `xhigh`,\n    30\t    the effort maps to `max` instead of being dropped or erroring out.\n    31\t    \"\"\"\n    32\t    options = dict(profile.generation_options)\n    33\t    effort = options.get(\"reasoning_effort\")\n    34\t    if isinstance(effort, str) and effort.lower() == \"xhigh\" and \\\n    35\t            profile.protocol in NO_XHIGH_PROTOCOLS:\n    36\t        options[\"reasoning_effort\"] = \"max\"\n    37\t        return options, f\"reasoning_effort xhigh -&gt; max ({profile.protocol})\"\n    38\t    return options, None\n    39\t\n    40\t\n    41\tdef resolve_profile(catalog: UserConfig, name: str) -&gt; ModelProfile:\n    42\t    if name not in catalog.models:\n    43\t        raise ModelConfigError(\n    44\t            f\"unknown model profile {name!r}; configure it under [models.{name}] \"\n    45\t            \"in ~/.config/teamagents/config.toml (copy examples/config.toml from the \"\n    46\t            \"repository as a starting point)\")\n    47\t    return catalog.models[name]\n    48\t\n    49\t\n    50\tdef _api_key(profile: ModelProfile) -&gt; str | None:\n    51\t    if profile.api_key_env:\n    52\t        key = os.environ.get(profile.api_key_env)\n    53\t        if not key:\n    54\t            raise ModelConfigError(\n    55\t                f\"model profile {profile.provider!r} needs environment variable \"\n    56\t                f\"{profile.api_key_env}; export it (secrets never enter TeamSpec)\")\n    57\t        return key\n    58\t    return None\n    59\t\n    60\t\n    61\tdef build_chat_model(profile: ModelProfile,\n    62\t                     overrides: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -&gt; BaseChatModel:\n    63\t    \"\"\"Build the chat model for one profile. Raises ModelConfigError on gaps.\"\"\"\n    64\t    normalized, _note = normalize_effort(profile)\n    65\t    options = {**normalized, **(overrides or {})}\n    66\t    common: dict[str, Any] = {\n    67\t        \"model\": profile.model,\n    68\t        \"timeout\": profile.timeout,\n    69\t        \"max_retries\": profile.max_retries,\n    70\t        **options,\n    71\t    }\n    72\t    key = _api_key(profile)\n    73\t    if key is not None:\n    74\t        common[\"api_key\"] = key\n    75\t    if profile.base_url:\n    76\t        common[\"base_url\"] = profile.base_url\n    77\t    match profile.protocol:\n    78\t        case \"anthropic\":\n    79\t            from langchain_anthropic import ChatAnthropic\n    80\t            return ChatAnthropic(**common)\n    81\t        case \"deepseek\":\n    82\t            from langchain_deepseek import ChatDeepSeek\n    83\t            return ChatDeepSeek(**common)\n    84\t        case _:\n    85\t            from langchain_openai import ChatOpenAI\n    86\t            return ChatOpenAI(**common)\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "## 1. 产品定义与首版范围\n\nTeamAgents 是运行于 Linux 的通用终端 Agent 产品，提供完整终端用户界面（TUI），支持持续对话、工具操作和团队管理。任务由用户自然语言提出，不绑定预设行业、代码工作流或固定角色数量。\n\n| 项目 | 首版要求 |\n|---|---|\n| 用户入口 | TUI；用户始终直接与 Leader 交互 |\n| 团队建立 | Leader 理解需求后自动组队；用户可以查看、调整；Leader 一人成队合法 |\n| 首版团队能力 | 委派、并行、讨论、观察者、共享空间、信息隔离、多模型、恢复、自然语言组队、动态拓扑全部包含 |\n| 团队变更 | 成员或用户提出需求，Leader 决策，运行时校验后自动生效，无需用户确认 |\n| 变更生效点 | 默认等待受影响成员当前回合结束；允许显式取消，停止后应用 |\n| 内置成员 | Deep Agents；按成员配置模型、工具和 Skills |\n| 外部成员 | 首版接入本机 Codex CLI；接受任务，返回进度和结果，由 Leader 代为协作 |\n| 模型服务 | OpenAI、Anthropic、Kimi、DeepSeek、GLM |\n| 工作目录 | 共享与隔离均支持，由 Leader 按任务选择；Git 项目支持 worktree |\n| 默认工具权限 | 预授权范围内自动执行，超出范围请求用户批准 |\n| 全自动模式 | 用户可主动开启，使用当前用户的系统权限，无需逐次批准 |\n| 执行中补充要求 | Leader 尽快处理；相关成员在安全边界接收调整，其他工作继续 |\n| 会话生命周期 | 同一会话保留团队与上下文；Leader 决定复用或调整；新会话默认隔离 |\n| Leader 可见范围 | 任务进度、结果、定向消息、获准观察的事件；成员私有上下文保持隔离 |\n| 工具范围 | 文件读写与搜索、终端命令、网页搜索与抓取、MCP、Skills |\n\n首版不包含原生 Windows/macOS 支持、分布式执行、远程成员协议、Claude Code/pi 适配器、浏览器自动化、多模态产品交互或插件市场。通用任务通过模型与已配置工具执行，不承诺所有任务都无需环境、凭据或外部服务。\n\n## 2. 架构职责与核心约束\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>
